## 2026-06-30 — Pre-launch audit batch (US-1418, US-1420) + CI unblock

Two HIGH pre-launch-audit stories, quality-first, plus a CI-blocking regression fix.

- US-1418 Price Suggestions error/failure state — NOW COMPLETE (passes:true).
  src/pages/price-suggestions.tsx migrated off the legacy useEffect/useState
  fetch (which ignored the { error } of all four Supabase calls and rendered a
  failed load as an empty "no suggestions" table) to useQuery + <QueryBoundary>,
  matching inventory.tsx. The queryFn THROWS on any { error } so an outage routes
  to <ErrorState> (retry = refetch, isRetrying = isFetching) instead of an empty
  table (AC2); the genuinely-empty case keeps its existing "No active inventory
  items" copy (AC3). The not-ready window (no workspace owner yet) is treated as
  loading, not empty.

- US-1420 Rate-limit + cache the public passport chain read — NOW COMPLETE
  (passes:true). main.ts adds a per-IP, fail-closed limiter on GET
  /api/passport/:slug (rateLimiter(20, 60s, "passport-read", { failClosed,
  methods:["GET"] })) consistent with the tag/claim budgets (AC1). GET-only +
  the single-segment :slug match means it never double-limits POST /claim or the
  multi-segment /tag/* and /garments/* paths (AC3). passport.ts adds a short
  per-replica in-memory TtlCache (30s TTL, bounded with eviction) that caches
  ONLY successful PII-free chain responses, so a same-slug flood is served from
  memory instead of re-running the ~6-query fan-out (AC2). Bogus-slug floods miss
  the cache and are governed by the limiter. New TtlCache unit coverage in
  tests/passport_test.ts.

- CI unblock (not a story): the latest main commit (US-1416) introduced a tsc
  error in src/pages/submission-detail.tsx — disputePhotos[i] is File|undefined

## 2026-06-30 — Pre-launch audit batch 2 (US-1429, US-1430)

Two more pre-launch-audit stories, quality-first, both fully unit-tested.

- US-1429 Overview/Kanban stage links actually filter the Inventory table —
  NOW COMPLETE (passes:true). The Overview pipeline grid, stat cards, and Kanban
  "+N more" navigated with ?status=<stage>, but the triage table (the default
  inventory mode, listings.tsx) only read ?tab/q/sort/filter — so every such
  link landed on the default tab. Added a pure statusParamToTab() mapping
  (extracted to src/pages/flipdesk/inventory-tabs.ts with the TabId type — kept
  OUT of the 2.5k-line listings.tsx so importing it for a test doesn't drag the
  whole page into the coverage denominator) and wired it into the table's
  initialTab (explicit ?tab= still wins; ?status= maps in; else default). Fixed
  the Overview "Total items" card's no-op ?view=all → ?status=all. Also folded
  the mid-grade `grading` stage into TO_LIST_STATUSES so it isn't stranded out
  of To List (the tab's own comment says every pre-listed stage belongs there),
  which also makes a ?status=grading link land on a tab that shows it. Test:
  status-param-to-tab.test.ts asserts every FLIPDESK_PIPELINE stage + each
  stat-card status resolves to the intended tab.

- US-1430 Preserve the attempted URL through the login redirect — NOW COMPLETE
  (passes:true). ProtectedRoute now captures the attempted location and redirects
  to /login?next=<path+search> instead of a bare /login. New src/lib/return-to.ts
  exports sanitizeReturnTo() — an open-redirect guard that accepts only same-
  origin internal paths (rejecting //host, /\host, encoded-slash, absolute URLs,
  control chars, and auth surfaces) — plus RETURN_TO_KEY. LoginPage navigates to
  the validated next on email sign-in (invite flow still special-cased), falling
  back to /dashboard; for OAuth it stashes next in sessionStorage (the provider
  round-trip drops the query param) and auth-callback consumes + clears it after
  the invite check. Test: return-to.test.ts covers the guard.

VERIFICATION (this container): frontend gate fully green — eslint (0 errors, 0
new warnings), `npx tsc -b` clean, vitest 1003 pass with coverage above all
floors (stmts 75.56/72, branches 65.68/65, funcs 74.36/68, lines 77.46/72),
`npm run build` + bundle-budget OK. No edge changes this batch. (Note: the
extraction to inventory-tabs.ts was specifically to keep coverage honest — a
first pass that imported listings.tsx directly collapsed the coverage % by
pulling the page's whole module graph into the denominator.)

## 2026-06-30 — Pre-launch audit batch 3 (US-1432, US-1436)

Two auth-error-handling stories (PR #147 already merged; this is a fresh branch
off the updated main per the merged-PR workflow). Both share a new helper.

- US-1432 Distinguish rate-limit / network errors from credential failures —
  NOW COMPLETE (passes:true). Login funneled every failure (incl. GoTrue 429 and
  offline) into the single SIGN_IN_FAILED_MESSAGE; signup collapsed every
  non-"already registered" error into one generic message. New src/lib/
  auth-error.ts exports classifyAuthFailure(err) → rate_limit (429 /
  over_request_rate_limit / over_email_send_rate_limit) | network (TypeError,
  AuthRetryableFetchError, status 0/502/503/504, fetch/network/timeout text) |
  credentials (the default, for genuine 400/401). LoginPage + SignupPage now
  surface distinct enumeration-safe messages (AUTH_RATE_LIMIT_MESSAGE /
  AUTH_NETWORK_ERROR_MESSAGE) for the first two and keep the generic credential
  message only for real auth failures. Test: auth-error.test.ts asserts each
  bucket, that rate_limit wins over a 4xx, and that unknown errors default to
  credentials (no false "you're offline").

- US-1436 Fail fast on an expired/invalid password-reset link — NOW COMPLETE
  (passes:true). readAuthError() (reads error/error_description from query AND
  hash) was extracted from auth-callback.tsx into the shared auth-error.ts (AC3
  reuse) — auth-callback now imports it, behavior unchanged. UpdatePasswordForm
  (reset-password.tsx) calls readAuthError() on mount and jumps straight to the
  expired/invalid state when the link carries an error, instead of spinning for
  the full 10s timeout; the timeout stays only as the no-session/no-error
  fallback (AC2).

VERIFICATION (this container): frontend gate fully green — eslint (0 errors/new
warnings), `npx tsc -b` clean, vitest 1008 pass with coverage above all floors
(stmts 75.48/72, branches 65.71/65, funcs 74.31/68, lines 77.37/72), `npm run
build` + bundle-budget OK. No edge changes this batch.

Open prd.json stories now 153 (down from 155).

## 2026-06-30 — Feature: edit & resubmit a published eBay listing from the web (US-1490)

User-requested (not from the audit backlog). The web composer already had eBay
category/specifics/condition editors + a publish flow; the missing piece was
editing an ALREADY-published listing and pushing the changes — including the
structured fields — back to eBay. (Verified via an Explore pass that capabilities
a–d already existed; only the post-publish edit/resubmit was absent.)

- EDGE: ebay-client.ts updateOfferFields() gains `categoryId` (the eBay leaf
  category lives on the offer, not the inventory item, so a category change must
  be re-asserted there). flipdesk-ebay.ts POST /listings/:id/revise gains a
  `resync_ebay_fields` flag: it widens the inventory-item re-PUT gate (which
  already rebuilds aspects from columns + auto-corrects condition against the
  category policy) and the offer-update gate (now pushing the lifted
  reviseCategoryId), and is folded into the at-least-one validation + the
  eBay-origin lock (category/condition/specifics are eBay-owned too).

- FRONTEND: useEbayReviseListing's ReviseListingPatch gains resync_ebay_fields.
  composer.tsx detects isLiveListing (listing_status=active + platform_offer_id)
  and renders a "live on eBay" banner + a "Save & resubmit to eBay" action in
  place of Publish. New saveLiveListing() updates the EXISTING active row in
  place (never inserts a draft, never flips listing_status/is_active) and mirrors
  category/aspects to the item WITHOUT regressing its 'listed' status;
  handleResubmitClick() saves then revises with { photos, resync_ebay_fields }.
  resolveListingFields() is shared by the draft + live save paths so they can't
  drift. item-canvas.tsx adds an "Edit eBay listing" overflow action for
  GradeThread-originated listed items (eBay-originated mirrors stay read-only and
  link out to eBay).

VERIFICATION (this container): frontend gate green — eslint (0 errors), tsc -b
clean, vitest 1008 pass, build + bundle-budget OK. Edge: deno lint clean on the
two changed files; deno check/test couldn't run (egress blocks esm.sh +
deno.land/x) — edge CI (security.yml deno-check) type-checks + tests it. eBay
API calls (offer category re-assert, inventory re-PUT) are exercised against the
sandbox by CI/manual QA, not in this container.

prd.json: added US-1490 (passes:true), nextId bumped 1490→1491.

## 2026-06-30 — Pre-launch audit batch 4 (US-1431, US-1461)

Two more audit stories (auth/onboarding), both frontend + fully locally verified.

- US-1431 Allow re-sending the confirmation email more than once — NOW COMPLETE
  (passes:true). VerifyEmailGate replaced the permanent `sent` lock (which left
  the button disabled reading "Email sent" forever) with a 45s ticking cooldown:
  a per-second useEffect decrements it, the label shows "Resend in Ns", and the
  button re-enables afterward so a user whose email never arrived can retry
  without reloading (AC1/AC2). AC3: a GoTrue 429 is classified via the
  classifyAuthFailure helper (shipped in US-1432) and surfaced with the distinct
  AUTH_RATE_LIMIT_MESSAGE, and it starts the cooldown so the user stops hammering
  the rate limit. New component test (createRoot+act + fake timers) covers the
  countdown/re-enable and the 429 path.

- US-1461 Fix the non-functional close button + missing ARIA on the onboarding
  modal — NOW COMPLETE (passes:true). onboarding-flow.tsx now passes
  showCloseButton={!!useCase} (the shadcn DialogContent already supports it), so
  the corner X — a dead control while the use case is required — is hidden until
  a use case is chosen, then appears and dismisses the skippable tour (AC1). The
  modal use-case buttons get aria-pressed={selected} matching the signup form
  (AC2), and a polite sr-only role=status region announces "Step N of 6: <title>"
  on each step change (AC3).
